src="https://cdn.choosechicago.com/uploads/svg/star.svg"/></span></p><h1 class="text-center section-title">Welcome to THE city that feels like home</h1><p class="lead text-center">Chicago is a city unlike any other. We’ve got the architectural marvels, world-class museums, dynamic entertainment, and award-winning dining scene you’d expect from one of the world’s greatest cities. But we’re more than just another big city — our welcoming residents, inclusive neighborhoods, and Midwestern soul truly make Chicago the city that feels like home.</p><p class="lead text-center">Explore our city, find things to do, start planning your trip, and discover your own unique Chicago style.</p><p> </p><div class="mt-n5 py-3 text-center"><img alt="" class="aligncenter img-fluid px-3 px-md-0 size-full wp-image-187916" height="150" src="https://cdn.choosechicago.com/uploads/2019/10/cnt-seal-140.png" width="150"/></div><p class="text-center h5">Voted Best Large City in the U.S. three years in a row!</p></div></div> <div class="card bg-light py-5"><div class="container"><div class="row mb-4"><div class="col-12 col-md-10 col-xl-9 mx-auto text-center"><h1 class="text-primary section-title" data-uk-scrollspy="{cls:'anim-slide-bottom', delay:200}">Find your Chicago style</h1> <p class="lead">There are tons of ways to experience Chicago.
